import { Link } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Boxes,
  CalendarCheck2,
  Clock,
  FolderKanban,
  Plus,
} from 'lucide-react';
import Badge from '@/components/common/Badge';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/common/Skeleton';
import { useBookingsQuery } from '@/hooks/queries/useBookings';
import { useCategoriesQuery } from '@/hooks/queries/useCategories';
import { useResourcesQuery } from '@/hooks/queries/useResources';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import { BOOKING_STATUS_META, formatCurrency, formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format';

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const user = useAuthStore((s) => s.user);
  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'ADMIN';

  const resourcesQuery = useResourcesQuery({ page: 1, limit: 5 });
  const bookingsQuery = useBookingsQuery({ page: 1, limit: 5 });
  const categoriesQuery = useCategoriesQuery({ page: 1, limit: 5 });

  const isLoading =
    resourcesQuery.isLoading || bookingsQuery.isLoading || categoriesQuery.isLoading;
  const queryError = resourcesQuery.error || bookingsQuery.error || categoriesQuery.error;

  const stats = [
    {
      title: 'Усього ресурсів',
      value: resourcesQuery.data?.pagination.total ?? 0,
      icon: Boxes,
      to: '/dashboard/resources',
      accent: 'bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 dark:bg-blue-950/60 dark:text-blue-400',
    },
    {
      title: 'Бронювання',
      value: bookingsQuery.data?.pagination.total ?? 0,
      icon: CalendarCheck2,
      to: '/dashboard/bookings',
      accent: 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-400',
    },
    {
      title: 'Категорії',
      value: categoriesQuery.data?.pagination.total ?? 0,
      icon: FolderKanban,
      to: '/dashboard/categories',
      accent: 'bg-purple-50 text-purple-600 dark:bg-purple-950/60 dark:text-purple-400',
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div>
      <Breadcrumbs items={[{ label: 'Панель керування' }]} />

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white sm:text-3xl">
            Вітаємо, {user?.name ?? 'користувачу'}!
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Огляд ресурсів, активних бронювань та швидкий доступ до основних розділів
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2.5">
          <Link to="/dashboard/bookings/new">
            <Button size="sm">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              Нове бронювання
            </Button>
          </Link>
          {isAdmin && (
            <Link to="/dashboard/resources/new">
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                Додати ресурс
              </Button>
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {queryError && (
        <ErrorAlert
          message={extractApiErrorMessage(queryError)}
          onRetry={() => {
            resourcesQuery.refetch();
            bookingsQuery.refetch();
            categoriesQuery.refetch();
          }}
          className="mb-6"
        />
      )}

      {/* Картки статистики */}
      <div className="mb-8 grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-3">
        {stats.map((stat) => {
          const Icon = stat.icon;
          return (
            <Link
              key={stat.title}
              to={stat.to}
              className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs transition hover:border-primary-300 hover:shadow-md dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:hover:border-primary-800"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className={`flex h-11 w-11 items-center justify-center rounded-xl ${stat.accent}`}>
                  <Icon className="h-5 w-5" />
                </div>
                <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4 text-slate-400 transition group-hover:translate-x-1 group-hover:text-primary-600" />
              </div>
              <p className="mt-4 text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                {stat.title}
              </p>
              {isLoading ? (
                <Skeleton className="mt-1.5 h-8 w-20" />
              ) : (
                <p className="mt-1 text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {stat.value}
                </p>
              )}
            </Link>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Останні бронювання та доступні ресурси */}
      <div className="grid gap-6 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Останні бронювання
            </h2>
            <Link
              to="/dashboard/bookings"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary-600 hover:underline dark:text-primary-400"
            >
              Усі бронювання →
            </Link>
          </div>

          {bookingsQuery.isLoading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Skeleton key={i} className="h-14 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (bookingsQuery.data?.data.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Бронювань поки що немає.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
              {bookingsQuery.data?.data.map((booking) => {
                const statusMeta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[booking.status] ?? {
                  label: booking.status,
                  variant: 'neutral' as const,
                };
                return (
                  <Link
                    key={booking.id}
                    to={`/dashboard/bookings/${booking.id}`}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-3 transition hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                        {booking.resource?.name ?? `Ресурс #${booking.resourceId}`}
                      </p>
                      <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                        <Clock className="h-3 w-3" />
                        <span>{formatDateTime(booking.startTime)}</span>
                      </p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge variant={statusMeta.variant}>{statusMeta.label}</Badge>
                  </Link>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>

        <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-5 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Популярні ресурси
            </h2>
            <Link
              to="/dashboard/resources"
              className="text-xs font-semibold text-primary-600 hover:underline dark:text-primary-400"
            >
              Усі ресурси →
            </Link>
          </div>

          {resourcesQuery.isLoading ? (
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Skeleton key={i} className="h-14 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : (resourcesQuery.data?.data.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
            <p className="py-8 text-center text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Ресурси відсутні.
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
              {resourcesQuery.data?.data.map((resource) => (
                <Link
                  key={resource.id}
                  to={`/dashboard/resources/${resource.id}`}
                  className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 py-3 transition hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
                >
                  <div className="min-w-0">
                    <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                      {resource.name}
                    </p>
                    <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                      {resource.category?.name ?? 'Без категорії'}
                      {resource.location ? ` • ${resource.location}` : ''}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                  <span className="shrink-0 text-sm font-bold text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400">
                    {formatCurrency(resource.price)}
                  </span>
                </Link>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </section>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: .\client\src\pages\HomePage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState, type SyntheticEvent } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import {
  bookingService,
  extractApiErrorMessage,
  tokenStorage,
} from '../services/api';
import type { Category, EntityId, PaginationMeta, Resource } from '../types';

export const HomePage = () => {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [resources, setResources] = useState<Resource[]>([]);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [pagination, setPagination] = useState<PaginationMeta | null>(null);

  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState<string>('');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [page, setPage] = useState<number>(1);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const [bookingResource, setBookingResource] = useState<Resource | null>(null);
  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState<string>('');
  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState<string>('');
  const [bookingFeedback, setBookingFeedback] = useState<{
    type: 'success' | 'error';
    text: string;
  } | null>(null);
  const [submittingBooking, setSubmittingBooking] = useState<boolean>(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    bookingService
      .getCategories()
      .then(setCategories)
      .catch(() => {
        // Категорії не критичні для базового рендеру каталогу
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true;

    bookingService
      .getResources({
        page,
        limit: 9,
        ...(searchQuery.trim() && { search: searchQuery.trim() }),
        ...(selectedCategory && { categoryId: selectedCategory }),
      })
      .then((response) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        setResources(response.data);
        setPagination(response.pagination);
        setError(null);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        setError(extractApiErrorMessage(err));
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (isMounted) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [page, searchQuery, selectedCategory]);

  const handleOpenBooking = (resource: Resource) => {
    if (!tokenStorage.getAccessToken()) {
      navigate('/login');
      return;
    }
    setBookingFeedback(null);
    setBookingResource(resource);
  };

  const handleCreateBooking = async (e: SyntheticEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!bookingResource) return;

    setSubmittingBooking(true);
    setBookingFeedback(null);

    try {
      await bookingService.createBooking({
        resourceId: bookingResource.id as EntityId,
        startTime: new Date(startTime).toISOString(),
        endTime: new Date(endTime).toISOString(),
      });
      setBookingFeedback({
        type: 'success',
        text: `Ресурс «${bookingResource.name}» успішно заброньовано!`,
      });
      setStartTime('');
      setEndTime('');
    } catch (err) {
      setBookingFeedback({
        type: 'error',
        text: extractApiErrorMessage(err),
      });
    } finally {
      setSubmittingBooking(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-8">
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col gap-4 md:flex-row md:items-end md:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
            Каталог ресурсів
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Оберіть локацію або обладнання та забронюйте зручний часовий слот
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setLoading(true);
              setPage(1);
              setSearchQuery(e.target.value);
            }}
            placeholder="Пошук за назвою або локацією..."
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-white"
          />

          <select
            value={selectedCategory}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setLoading(true);
              setPage(1);
              setSelectedCategory(e.target.value);
            }}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3.5 py-2 text-sm text-slate-900 focus:border-blue-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-white"
          >
            <option value="">Усі категорії</option>
            {categories.map((cat) => (
              <option key={cat.id} value={String(cat.id)}>
                {cat.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      {error && (
        <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 dark:border-red-900/50 dark:bg-red-950/50 dark:text-red-300">
          {error}
        </div>
      )}

      {loading ? (
        <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, idx) => (
            <div
              key={idx}
              className="h-56 animate-pulse rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-800/60"
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : resources.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-12 text-center text-slate-500 dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900 dark:text-slate-400">
          За вашим запитом ресурсів не знайдено.
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {resources.map((resource) => (
            <article
              key={resource.id}
              className="flex flex-col justify-between rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm transition hover:shadow-md dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
            >
              <div>
                <div className="mb-3 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                  <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950 dark:text-blue-300">
                    {resource.category?.name || 'Загальна категорія'}
                  </span>
                  <span className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                    {Number(resource.price).toFixed(2)} ₴
                  </span>
                </div>

                <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                  {resource.name}
                </h2>

                {resource.location && (
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    Локація: {resource.location}
                  </p>
                )}

                {resource.description && (
                  <p className="mt-3 line-clamp-3 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                    {resource.description}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => handleOpenBooking(resource)}
                className="mt-6 w-full rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition hover:bg-blue-700"
              >
                Забронювати
              </button>
            </article>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {pagination && pagination.totalPages > 1 && (
        <div className="mt-8 flex items-center justify-center gap-4">
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!pagination.hasPreviousPage}
            onClick={() => {
              setLoading(true);
              setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1));
            }}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-40 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-white"
          >
            Назад
          </button>
          <span className="text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Сторінка {pagination.page} з {pagination.totalPages}
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            disabled={!pagination.hasNextPage}
            onClick={() => {
              setLoading(true);
              setPage((p) => p + 1);
            }}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-40 dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-white"
          >
            Далі
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {bookingResource && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl dark:bg-slate-900">
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              Бронювання: {bookingResource.name}
            </h3>

            {bookingFeedback && (
              <div
                className={`mt-4 rounded-lg p-3 text-sm ${
                  bookingFeedback.type === 'success'
                    ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300'
                    : 'bg-red-50 text-red-700 dark:bg-red-950/60 dark:text-red-300'
                }`}
              >
                {bookingFeedback.text}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleCreateBooking} className="mt-4 space-y-4">
              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  Початок
                </label>
                <input
                  type="datetime-local"
                  required
                  value={startTime}
                  onChange={(e) => setStartTime(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label className="mb-1 block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                  Завершення
                </label>
                <input
                  type="datetime-local"
                  required
                  value={endTime}
                  onChange={(e) => setEndTime(e.target.value)}
                  className="w-full rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-3 py-2 text-sm dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-white"
                />
              </div>

              <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setBookingResource(null)}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 px-4 py-2 text-sm dark:border-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300"
                >
                  Закрити
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={submittingBooking}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  {submittingBooking ? 'Збереження...' : 'Підтвердити'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default HomePage;


================================================================================
FILE: .\client\src\pages\LoginPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { LogIn } from 'lucide-react';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Input from '@/components/common/Input';
import { loginSchema, type LoginFormValues } from '@/schemas/validation';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import { useToast } from '@/stores/uiStore';

export default function LoginPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();
  const login = useAuthStore((s) => s.login);
  const error = useAuthStore((s) => s.error);
  const clearError = useAuthStore((s) => s.clearError);
  const toast = useToast();

  const from =
    (location.state as { from?: { pathname?: string } } | null)?.from?.pathname || '/dashboard';

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
  } = useForm<LoginFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(loginSchema),
    defaultValues: { email: '', password: '' },
  });

  const onSubmit = async (values: LoginFormValues) => {
    try {
      await login(values.email, values.password);
      toast.success('Вхід виконано успішно');
      navigate(from, { replace: true });
    } catch {
      // Помилка вже збережена в authStore
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto my-10 max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary-100 text-primary-600 dark:bg-primary-950 dark:text-primary-400">
          <LogIn className="h-5 w-5" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Вхід у систему</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Авторизуйтесь для доступу до панелі керування
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ErrorAlert message={error} onDismiss={clearError} className="mb-5" />

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate className="space-y-4">
        <Input
          label="Електронна пошта"
          type="email"
          placeholder="user@example.com"
          error={errors.email?.message}
          {...register('email')}
        />

        <Input
          label="Пароль"
          type="password"
          placeholder="••••••••"
          error={errors.password?.message}
          {...register('password')}
        />

        <Button type="submit" isLoading={isSubmitting} className="w-full">
          Увійти
        </Button>
      </form>

      <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
        Ще не маєте облікового запису?{' '}
        <Link
          to="/register"
          onClick={clearError}
          className="font-semibold text-primary-600 hover:underline dark:text-primary-400"
        >
          Зареєструватися
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: .\client\src\pages\RegisterPage.tsx

import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { UserPlus } from 'lucide-react';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Input from '@/components/common/Input';
import { registerSchema, type RegisterFormValues } from '@/schemas/validation';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import { useToast } from '@/stores/uiStore';

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const registerUser = useAuthStore((s) => s.register);
  const error = useAuthStore((s) => s.error);
  const clearError = useAuthStore((s) => s.clearError);
  const toast = useToast();

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
  } = useForm<RegisterFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(registerSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      name: '',
      email: '',
      password: '',
      confirmPassword: '',
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = async (values: RegisterFormValues) => {
    try {
      await registerUser(values.name, values.email, values.password);
      if (useAuthStore.getState().isAuthenticated) {
        toast.success('Реєстрацію завершено! Ласкаво просимо.');
        navigate('/dashboard', { replace: true });
      } else {
        toast.success('Обліковий запис створено. Тепер увійдіть у систему.');
        navigate('/login', { replace: true });
      }
    } catch {
      // Помилка відображається через ErrorAlert
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto my-10 max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-8 shadow-sm dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-primary-100 text-primary-600 dark:bg-primary-950 dark:text-primary-400">
          <UserPlus className="h-5 w-5" />
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Реєстрація</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            Створіть обліковий запис у BookingSystem
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ErrorAlert message={error} onDismiss={clearError} className="mb-5" />

      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate className="space-y-4">
        <Input
          label="Ім’я та прізвище"
          type="text"
          placeholder="Олександр Коваленко"
          error={errors.name?.message}
          {...register('name')}
        />

        <Input
          label="Електронна пошта"
          type="email"
          placeholder="user@example.com"
          error={errors.email?.message}
          {...register('email')}
        />

        <Input
          label="Пароль"
          type="password"
          placeholder="Мінімум 6 символів"
          error={errors.password?.message}
          {...register('password')}
        />

        <Input
          label="Підтвердження пароля"
          type="password"
          placeholder="Повторіть пароль"
          error={errors.confirmPassword?.message}
          {...register('confirmPassword')}
        />

        <Button type="submit" isLoading={isSubmitting} className="w-full">
          Створити обліковий запис
        </Button>
      </form>

      <p className="mt-6 text-center text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
        Вже зареєстровані?{' '}
        <Link
          to="/login"
          onClick={clearError}
          className="font-semibold text-primary-600 hover:underline dark:text-primary-400"
        >
          Увійти
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: .\client\src\pages\bookings\BookingDetailsPage.tsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Boxes, Calendar, Clock, Pencil, Trash2, User } from 'lucide-react';
import Badge from '@/components/common/Badge';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ConfirmModal from '@/components/common/ConfirmModal';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import { DetailsSkeleton } from '@/components/common/Skeleton';
import { useBookingQuery, useDeleteBookingMutation } from '@/hooks/queries/useBookings';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import { BOOKING_STATUS_META, formatCurrency, formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format';

export default function BookingDetailsPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [isDeleteOpen, setIsDeleteOpen] = useState(false);

  const bookingQuery = useBookingQuery(id);
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteBookingMutation();

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!id) return;
    await deleteMutation.mutateAsync(id);
    navigate('/dashboard/bookings', { replace: true });
  };

  if (bookingQuery.isLoading) {
    return <DetailsSkeleton />;
  }

  if (bookingQuery.error || !bookingQuery.data) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Breadcrumbs currentLabel="Бронювання не знайдено" />
        <ErrorAlert
          message={extractApiErrorMessage(
            bookingQuery.error ?? new Error('Бронювання не знайдено')
          )}
          onRetry={() => bookingQuery.refetch()}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const booking = bookingQuery.data;
  const statusMeta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[booking.status];

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Панель керування', to: '/dashboard' },
          { label: 'Бронювання', to: '/dashboard/bookings' },
          { label: `Бронювання #${booking.id}` },
        ]}
      />

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-6 dark:border-slate-800 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
              <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                Бронювання #{booking.id}
              </h1>
              <Badge variant={statusMeta.variant}>{statusMeta.label}</Badge>
            </div>
            {booking.createdAt && (
              <p className="mt-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                Створено: {formatDateTime(booking.createdAt)}
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            <Link to="/dashboard/bookings">
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                До списку
              </Button>
            </Link>
            <Link to={`/dashboard/bookings/${booking.id}/edit`}>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm">
                <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
                Редагувати
              </Button>
            </Link>
            <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={() => setIsDeleteOpen(true)}>
              <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
              Видалити
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <Boxes className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Ресурс
            </p>
            <Link
              to={`/dashboard/resources/${booking.resourceId}`}
              className="mt-1 block text-sm font-semibold text-primary-600 hover:underline dark:text-primary-400"
            >
              {booking.resource?.name ?? `Ресурс #${booking.resourceId}`}
            </Link>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <Calendar className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Початок
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {formatDateTime(booking.startTime)}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Завершення
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {formatDateTime(booking.endTime)}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="rounded-xl bg-slate-50 p-4 dark:bg-slate-800/50">
            <p className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              <User className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Замовник / Вартість
            </p>
            <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
              {booking.user?.name ?? 'Поточний користувач'}
              {booking.totalPrice ? ` • ${formatCurrency(booking.totalPrice)}` : ''}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-6">
          <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Примітки</h2>
          <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
            {booking.notes || 'Додаткових приміток не вказано.'}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ConfirmModal
        isOpen={isDeleteOpen}
        title="Видалити бронювання?"
        description={`Ви впевнені, що бажаєте остаточно видалити бронювання #${booking.id}?`}
        isLoading={deleteMutation.isPending}
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
        onCancel={() => setIsDeleteOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: .\client\src\pages\bookings\BookingFormPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Input, { Select, Textarea } from '@/components/common/Input';
import Loading from '@/components/common/Loading';
import {
  useBookingQuery,
  useCreateBookingMutation,
  useUpdateBookingMutation,
} from '@/hooks/queries/useBookings';
import { useResourcesQuery } from '@/hooks/queries/useResources';
import { bookingSchema, type BookingFormValues } from '@/schemas/validation';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import { formatCurrency, toDateTimeLocalValue } from '@/utils/format';

export default function BookingFormPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const [searchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const preselectedResourceId = searchParams.get('resourceId') ?? '';
  const isEditMode = Boolean(id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const isAdmin = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role === 'ADMIN');

  const bookingQuery = useBookingQuery(id);
  const resourcesQuery = useResourcesQuery({ page: 1, limit: 100 });
  const createMutation = useCreateBookingMutation();
  const updateMutation = useUpdateBookingMutation(id ?? '');

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<BookingFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(bookingSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      resourceId: preselectedResourceId,
      startTime: '',
      endTime: '',
      status: 'PENDING',
      notes: '',
    },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isEditMode && bookingQuery.data) {
      const b = bookingQuery.data;
      reset({
        resourceId: String(b.resourceId),
        startTime: toDateTimeLocalValue(b.startTime),
        endTime: toDateTimeLocalValue(b.endTime),
        status: b.status,
        notes: b.notes ?? '',
      });
    } else if (!isEditMode && preselectedResourceId) {
      reset((prev) => ({ ...prev, resourceId: preselectedResourceId }));
    }
  }, [isEditMode, bookingQuery.data, preselectedResourceId, reset]);

  const onSubmit = async (values: BookingFormValues) => {
    if (isEditMode && id) {
      await updateMutation.mutateAsync({
        startTime: new Date(values.startTime).toISOString(),
        endTime: new Date(values.endTime).toISOString(),
        ...(values.status ? { status: values.status } : {}),
      });
    } else {
      await createMutation.mutateAsync({
        resourceId: Number(values.resourceId),
        startTime: new Date(values.startTime).toISOString(),
        endTime: new Date(values.endTime).toISOString(),
        ...(values.notes ? { notes: values.notes } : {}),
      });
    }
    navigate('/dashboard/bookings');
  };

  if (isEditMode && bookingQuery.isLoading) {
    return <Loading text="Завантаження даних бронювання..." />;
  }

  const mutationError = createMutation.error || updateMutation.error || bookingQuery.error;
  const isSaving = createMutation.isPending || updateMutation.isPending;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-2xl">
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Панель керування', to: '/dashboard' },
          { label: 'Бронювання', to: '/dashboard/bookings' },
          { label: isEditMode ? `Редагування #${id}` : 'Нове бронювання' },
        ]}
      />

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
          {isEditMode ? `Редагування бронювання #${id}` : 'Створення нового бронювання'}
        </h1>
        <Link to="/dashboard/bookings">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Назад
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {mutationError && (
        <ErrorAlert message={extractApiErrorMessage(mutationError)} className="mb-5" />
      )}

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        noValidate
        className="space-y-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
      >
      <Select
          label="Ресурс для бронювання *"
          className={isEditMode ? 'pointer-events-none opacity-60' : ''}
          tabIndex={isEditMode ? -1 : undefined}
          error={errors.resourceId?.message}
          {...register('resourceId')}
        >
          <option value="">-- Оберіть ресурс --</option>
          {resourcesQuery.data?.data.map((res) => (
            <option key={res.id} value={String(res.id)}>
              {res.name} ({formatCurrency(res.price)}/год)
            </option>
          ))}
        </Select>

        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Input
            label="Дата та час початку *"
            type="datetime-local"
            error={errors.startTime?.message}
            {...register('startTime')}
          />

          <Input
            label="Дата та час завершення *"
            type="datetime-local"
            error={errors.endTime?.message}
            {...register('endTime')}
          />
        </div>

        {isEditMode && (
          <Select label="Статус бронювання" error={errors.status?.message} {...register('status')}>
            <option value="PENDING">Очікує підтвердження</option>
            {isAdmin && <option value="CONFIRMED">Підтверджено</option>}
            {isAdmin && <option value="COMPLETED">Завершено</option>}
            <option value="CANCELLED">Скасовано</option>
          </Select>
        )}

        <Textarea
          label="Коментар або побажання"
          rows={3}
          placeholder="Додаткова інформація щодо бронювання..."
          error={errors.notes?.message}
          {...register('notes')}
        />

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 dark:border-slate-800">
          <Link to="/dashboard/bookings">
            <Button type="button" variant="outline">
              Скасувати
            </Button>
          </Link>
          <Button type="submit" isLoading={isSaving}>
            <Save className="h-4 w-4" />
            {isEditMode ? 'Оновити бронювання' : 'Підтвердити бронювання'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: .\client\src\pages\bookings\BookingsListPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Clock, Eye, Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Badge from '@/components/common/Badge';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import Card from '@/components/common/Card';
import ConfirmModal from '@/components/common/ConfirmModal';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Pagination from '@/components/common/Pagination';
import { GridSkeleton, ListSkeleton, TableSkeleton } from '@/components/common/Skeleton';
import DataGrid from '@/components/data-display/DataGrid';
import DataList from '@/components/data-display/DataList';
import DataTable, { type TableColumn } from '@/components/data-display/DataTable';
import FilterBar from '@/components/data-display/FilterBar';
import ViewModeToggle, { type ViewMode } from '@/components/data-display/ViewModeToggle';
import { useBookingsQuery, useDeleteBookingMutation } from '@/hooks/queries/useBookings';
import { useDebounce } from '@/hooks/useDebounce';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import type { Booking, SortOrder } from '@/types';
import { BOOKING_STATUS_META, formatCurrency, formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format';

export default function BookingsListPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>('table');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(9);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState('startTime');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<SortOrder>('desc');
  const [bookingToDelete, setBookingToDelete] = useState<Booking | null>(null);

  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 350);

  const bookingsQuery = useBookingsQuery({
    page,
    limit,
    ...(debouncedSearch.trim() && { search: debouncedSearch.trim() }),
    ...(status && { status }),
    sortBy,
    sortOrder,
  });

  const deleteMutation = useDeleteBookingMutation();

  const sortedBookings = useMemo(() => {
    const list = [...(bookingsQuery.data?.data ?? [])];
    list.sort((a, b) => {
      let cmp: number;
if (sortBy === 'status') {
  cmp = a.status.localeCompare(b.status);
} else if (sortBy === 'createdAt') {
  cmp = new Date(a.createdAt ?? 0).getTime() - new Date(b.createdAt ?? 0).getTime();
} else {
  cmp = new Date(a.startTime).getTime() - new Date(b.startTime).getTime();
}
return sortOrder === 'asc' ? cmp : -cmp;
    });
    return list;
  }, [bookingsQuery.data?.data, sortBy, sortOrder]);

  const handleHeaderSort = (key: string) => {
    if (sortBy === key) {
      setSortOrder((prev) => (prev === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'));
    } else {
      setSortBy(key);
      setSortOrder('asc');
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!bookingToDelete) return;
    await deleteMutation.mutateAsync(bookingToDelete.id);
    setBookingToDelete(null);
  };

  const renderActions = (booking: Booking) => (
    <div
      className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5"
      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
    >
      <Link
        to={`/dashboard/bookings/${booking.id}`}
        title="Детальніше"
        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-600 transition hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
      >
        <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
      </Link>
      <Link
        to={`/dashboard/bookings/${booking.id}/edit`}
        title="Редагувати"
        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-primary-600 transition hover:bg-primary-50 dark:text-primary-400 dark:hover:bg-primary-950/50"
      >
        <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
      </Link>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setBookingToDelete(booking)}
        title="Видалити"
        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-50 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-950/50"
      >
        <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
      </button>
    </div>
  );

  const columns: TableColumn<Booking>[] = [
    {
      key: 'resource',
      header: 'Ресурс',
      render: (b) => (
        <div>
          <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
            {b.resource?.name ?? `Ресурс #${b.resourceId}`}
          </p>
          {b.user && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Користувач: {b.user.name}</p>}
        </div>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'startTime',
      header: 'Початок',
      sortKey: 'startTime',
      render: (b) => formatDateTime(b.startTime),
    },
    {
      key: 'endTime',
      header: 'Завершення',
      render: (b) => formatDateTime(b.endTime),
    },
    {
      key: 'status',
      header: 'Статус',
      sortKey: 'status',
      render: (b) => {
        const meta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[b.status];
        return <Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>;
      },
    },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: 'Дії',
      className: 'text-right',
      render: renderActions,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div>
      <Breadcrumbs />

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">Бронювання</h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Перегляд, створення та керування часовими слотами бронювання
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ViewModeToggle value={viewMode} onChange={setViewMode} />
          <Link to="/dashboard/bookings/new">
            <Button size="sm">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
              Створити бронювання
            </Button>
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <FilterBar
        search={search}
        onSearchChange={(val) => {
          setSearch(val);
          setPage(1);
        }}
        searchPlaceholder="Пошук за ресурсом або приміткою..."
        sortBy={sortBy}
        sortOrder={sortOrder}
        sortOptions={[
          { value: 'startTime', label: 'Датою початку' },
          { value: 'createdAt', label: 'Датою створення' },
          { value: 'status', label: 'Статусом' },
        ]}
        onSortByChange={setSortBy}
        onSortOrderToggle={() => setSortOrder((o) => (o === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'))}
        hasActiveFilters={Boolean(search || status)}
        onReset={() => {
          setSearch('');
          setStatus('');
          setSortBy('startTime');
          setSortOrder('desc');
          setPage(1);
        }}
        extraFilters={
          <select
            aria-label="Фільтр за статусом бронювання"
            value={status}
            onChange={(e) => {
              setStatus(e.target.value);
              setPage(1);
            }}
            className="rounded-lg border border-slate-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm text-slate-700 focus:border-primary-500 focus:outline-none dark:border-slate-700 dark:bg-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200"
          >
            <option value="">Усі статуси</option>
            <option value="PENDING">Очікує підтвердження</option>
            <option value="CONFIRMED">Підтверджено</option>
            <option value="COMPLETED">Завершено</option>
            <option value="CANCELLED">Скасовано</option>
          </select>
        }
      />

      {bookingsQuery.error && (
        <ErrorAlert
          message={extractApiErrorMessage(bookingsQuery.error)}
          onRetry={() => bookingsQuery.refetch()}
          className="mb-5"
        />
      )}

      {bookingsQuery.isLoading ? (
        viewMode === 'table' ? (
          <TableSkeleton rows={limit} cols={5} />
        ) : viewMode === 'list' ? (
          <ListSkeleton rows={limit} />
        ) : (
          <GridSkeleton count={limit} />
        )
      ) : viewMode === 'table' ? (
        <DataTable
          data={sortedBookings}
          columns={columns}
          keyExtractor={(b) => b.id}
          sortBy={sortBy}
          sortOrder={sortOrder}
          onSort={handleHeaderSort}
          onRowClick={(b) => navigate(`/dashboard/bookings/${b.id}`)}
          emptyMessage="Бронювань не знайдено."
        />
      ) : viewMode === 'list' ? (
        <DataList
          data={sortedBookings}
          keyExtractor={(b) => b.id}
          emptyMessage="Бронювань не знайдено."
          renderItem={(b) => {
            const meta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[b.status];
            return (
              <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
                <div>
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                    <Link
                      to={`/dashboard/bookings/${b.id}`}
                      className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white"
                    >
                      {b.resource?.name ?? `Ресурс #${b.resourceId}`}
                    </Link>
                    <Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <p className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    <Clock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>
                      {formatDateTime(b.startTime)} — {formatDateTime(b.endTime)}
                    </span>
                  </p>
                </div>
                {renderActions(b)}
              </div>
            );
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <DataGrid
          data={sortedBookings}
          keyExtractor={(b) => b.id}
          emptyMessage="Бронювань не знайдено."
          renderCard={(b) => {
            const meta = BOOKING_STATUS_META[b.status];
            return (
              <Card
                title={b.resource?.name ?? `Ресурс #${b.resourceId}`}
                subtitle={`Бронювання #${b.id}`}
                headerAction={<Badge variant={meta.variant}>{meta.label}</Badge>}
                className="h-full"
                footer={
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300">
                      {b.totalPrice ? formatCurrency(b.totalPrice) : ''}
                    </span>
                    {renderActions(b)}
                  </div>
                }
              >
                <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                  <p>
                    <strong>Початок:</strong> {formatDateTime(b.startTime)}
                  </p>
                  <p>
                    <strong>Завершення:</strong> {formatDateTime(b.endTime)}
                  </p>
                  {b.notes && <p className="mt-2 line-clamp-2 italic">«{b.notes}»</p>}
                </div>
              </Card>
            );
          }}
        />
      )}

      <Pagination
        pagination={bookingsQuery.data?.pagination}
        onPageChange={setPage}
        onLimitChange={(newLimit) => {
          setLimit(newLimit);
          setPage(1);
        }}
      />

      <ConfirmModal
        isOpen={Boolean(bookingToDelete)}
        title="Видалити бронювання?"
        description={`Ви дійсно бажаєте видалити бронювання #${bookingToDelete?.id ?? ''}?`}
        isLoading={deleteMutation.isPending}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmDelete}
        onCancel={() => setBookingToDelete(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: .\client\src\pages\categories\CategoriesListPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';
import { Eye, Pencil, Plus, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import Card from '@/components/common/Card';
import ConfirmModal from '@/components/common/ConfirmModal';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Pagination from '@/components/common/Pagination';
import { GridSkeleton, ListSkeleton, TableSkeleton } from '@/components/common/Skeleton';
import DataGrid from '@/components/data-display/DataGrid';
import DataList from '@/components/data-display/DataList';
import DataTable, { type TableColumn } from '@/components/data-display/DataTable';
import FilterBar from '@/components/data-display/FilterBar';
import ViewModeToggle, { type ViewMode } from '@/components/data-display/ViewModeToggle';
import { useCategoriesQuery, useDeleteCategoryMutation } from '@/hooks/queries/useCategories';
import { useDebounce } from '@/hooks/useDebounce';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import type { Category, SortOrder } from '@/types';
import { formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format';

export default function CategoriesListPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const isAdmin = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role === 'ADMIN');

  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState<ViewMode>('table');
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(9);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState('name');
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState<SortOrder>('asc');
  const [categoryToDelete, setCategoryToDelete] = useState<Category | null>(null);

  const debouncedSearch = useDebounce(search, 350);

  const categoriesQuery = useCategoriesQuery({
    page,
    limit,
    ...(debouncedSearch.trim() && { search: debouncedSearch.trim() }),
    sortBy,
    sortOrder,
  });

  const deleteMutation = useDeleteCategoryMutation();

  const sortedCategories = useMemo(() => {
    const list = [...(categoriesQuery.data?.data ?? [])];
    list.sort((a, b) => {
      const cmp =
        sortBy === 'createdAt'
          ? new Date(a.createdAt ?? 0).getTime() - new Date(b.createdAt ?? 0).getTime()
          : a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'uk');
      return sortOrder === 'asc' ? cmp : -cmp;
    });
    return list;
  }, [categoriesQuery.data?.data, sortBy, sortOrder]);

  const handleConfirmDelete = async () => {
    if (!categoryToDelete) return;
    await deleteMutation.mutateAsync(categoryToDelete.id);
    setCategoryToDelete(null);
  };

  const renderActions = (cat: Category) => (
    <div
      className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5"
      onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
    >
      <Link
        to={`/dashboard/categories/${cat.id}`}
        title="Детальніше"
        className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-slate-600 transition hover:bg-slate-100 dark:text-slate-300 dark:hover:bg-slate-800"
      >
        <Eye className="h-4 w-4" />
      </Link>
      {isAdmin && (
        <>
          <Link
            to={`/dashboard/categories/${cat.id}/edit`}
            title="Редагувати"
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-primary-600 transition hover:bg-primary-50 dark:text-primary-400 dark:hover:bg-primary-950/50"
          >
            <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Link>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setCategoryToDelete(cat)}
            title="Видалити"
            className="rounded-lg p-1.5 text-red-600 transition hover:bg-red-50 dark:text-red-400 dark:hover:bg-red-950/50"
          >
            <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
          </button>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );

  const columns: TableColumn<Category>[] = [
    {
      key: 'name',
      header: 'Назва категорії',
      sortKey: 'name',
      render: (c) => <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{c.name}</span>,
    },
    {
      key: 'description',
      header: 'Опис',
      render: (c) => (
        <span className="line-clamp-2 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
          {c.description || '—'}
        </span>
      ),
    },
    {
      key: 'createdAt',
      header: 'Створено',
      sortKey: 'createdAt',
      render: (c) => formatDateTime(c.createdAt),
    },
    {
      key: 'actions',
      header: 'Дії',
      className: 'text-right',
      render: renderActions,
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div>
      <Breadcrumbs />

      <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
            Категорії ресурсів
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
            Класифікація ресурсів для зручного пошуку та фільтрації
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
          <ViewModeToggle value={viewMode} onChange={setViewMode} />
          {isAdmin && (
            <Link to="/dashboard/categories/new">
              <Button size="sm">
                <Plus className="h-4 w-4" />
                Нова категорія
              </Button>
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <FilterBar
        search={search}
        onSearchChange={(val) => {
          setSearch(val);
          setPage(1);
        }}
        searchPlaceholder="Пошук категорій..."
        sortBy={sortBy}
        sortOrder={sortOrder}
        sortOptions={[
          { value: 'name', label: 'Назвою' },
          { value: 'createdAt', label: 'Датою створення' },
        ]}
        onSortByChange={setSortBy}
        onSortOrderToggle={() => setSortOrder((o) => (o === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'))}
        hasActiveFilters={Boolean(search)}
        onReset={() => {
          setSearch('');
          setSortBy('name');
          setSortOrder('asc');
          setPage(1);
        }}
      />

      {categoriesQuery.error && (
        <ErrorAlert
          message={extractApiErrorMessage(categoriesQuery.error)}
          onRetry={() => categoriesQuery.refetch()}
          className="mb-5"
        />
      )}

      {categoriesQuery.isLoading ? (
        viewMode === 'table' ? (
          <TableSkeleton rows={limit} cols={4} />
        ) : viewMode === 'list' ? (
          <ListSkeleton rows={limit} />
        ) : (
          <GridSkeleton count={limit} />
        )
      ) : viewMode === 'table' ? (
        <DataTable
          data={sortedCategories}
          columns={columns}
          keyExtractor={(c) => c.id}
          sortBy={sortBy}
          sortOrder={sortOrder}
          onSort={(key) => {
            if (sortBy === key) setSortOrder((o) => (o === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc'));
            else {
              setSortBy(key);
              setSortOrder('asc');
            }
          }}
          onRowClick={(c) => navigate(`/dashboard/categories/${c.id}`)}
          emptyMessage="Категорій не знайдено."
        />
      ) : viewMode === 'list' ? (
        <DataList
          data={sortedCategories}
          keyExtractor={(c) => c.id}
          emptyMessage="Категорій не знайдено."
          renderItem={(c) => (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <Link
                  to={`/dashboard/categories/${c.id}`}
                  className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white"
                >
                  {c.name}
                </Link>
                {c.description && (
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                    {c.description}
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
              {renderActions(c)}
            </div>
          )}
        />
      ) : (
        <DataGrid
          data={sortedCategories}
          keyExtractor={(c) => c.id}
          emptyMessage="Категорій не знайдено."
          renderCard={(c) => (
            <Card
              title={c.name}
              subtitle={`ID #${c.id}`}
              className="h-full"
              footer={<div className="flex justify-end">{renderActions(c)}</div>}
            >
              <p className="line-clamp-3 text-sm text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-400">
                {c.description || 'Опис категорії відсутній.'}
              </p>
            </Card>
          )}
        />
      )}

      <Pagination
        pagination={categoriesQuery.data?.pagination}
        onPageChange={setPage}
        onLimitChange={(newLimit) => {
          setLimit(newLimit);
          setPage(1);
        }}
      />

      <ConfirmModal
        isOpen={Boolean(categoryToDelete)}
        title="Видалити категорію?"
        description={`Ви дійсно бажаєте видалити категорію «${categoryToDelete?.name ?? ''}»?`}
        isLoading={deleteMutation.isPending}
        onConfirm={handleConfirmDelete}
        onCancel={() => setCategoryToDelete(null)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: .\client\src\pages\categories\CategoryDetailsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Pencil, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ConfirmModal from '@/components/common/ConfirmModal';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import { DetailsSkeleton } from '@/components/common/Skeleton';
import { useCategoryQuery, useDeleteCategoryMutation } from '@/hooks/queries/useCategories';
import { useResourcesQuery } from '@/hooks/queries/useResources';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';
import { useAuthStore } from '@/stores/authStore';
import { formatCurrency, formatDateTime } from '@/utils/format';

export default function CategoryDetailsPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const isAdmin = useAuthStore((s) => s.user?.role === 'ADMIN');
  const [isDeleteOpen, setIsDeleteOpen] = useState(false);

  const categoryQuery = useCategoryQuery(id);
  const resourcesQuery = useResourcesQuery({
    page: 1,
    limit: 20,
    ...(id && { categoryId: id }),
  });
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteCategoryMutation();

  const handleDelete = async () => {
    if (!id) return;
    await deleteMutation.mutateAsync(id);
    navigate('/dashboard/categories', { replace: true });
  };

  if (categoryQuery.isLoading) {
    return <DetailsSkeleton />;
  }

  if (categoryQuery.error || !categoryQuery.data) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-4">
        <Breadcrumbs currentLabel="Категорію не знайдено" />
        <ErrorAlert
          message={extractApiErrorMessage(
            categoryQuery.error ?? new Error('Категорію не знайдено')
          )}
          onRetry={() => categoryQuery.refetch()}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const category = categoryQuery.data;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Панель керування', to: '/dashboard' },
          { label: 'Категорії', to: '/dashboard/categories' },
          { label: category.name },
        ]}
      />

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-4 border-b border-slate-100 pb-6 dark:border-slate-800 sm:flex-row sm:items-start sm:justify-between">
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{category.name}</h1>
            <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              ID: #{category.id}
              {category.createdAt ? ` • Створено: ${formatDateTime(category.createdAt)}` : ''}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2.5">
            <Link to="/dashboard/categories">
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
                <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
                До списку
              </Button>
            </Link>
            {isAdmin && (
              <>
                <Link to={`/dashboard/categories/${category.id}/edit`}>
                  <Button variant="secondary" size="sm">
                    <Pencil className="h-4 w-4" />
                    Редагувати
                  </Button>
                </Link>
                <Button variant="danger" size="sm" onClick={() => setIsDeleteOpen(true)}>
                  <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Видалити
                </Button>
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <p className="mt-5 text-sm leading-relaxed text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
          {category.description || 'Опис для цієї категорії не вказано.'}
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900">
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
          Ресурси у цій категорії ({resourcesQuery.data?.pagination.total ?? 0})
        </h2>

        {(resourcesQuery.data?.data.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
            У цій категорії поки що немає жодного ресурсу.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-slate-100 dark:divide-slate-800">
            {resourcesQuery.data?.data.map((res) => (
              <Link
                key={res.id}
                to={`/dashboard/resources/${res.id}`}
                className="flex items-center justify-between py-3 text-sm hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-slate-800/40"
              >
                <div>
                  <p className="font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{res.name}</p>
                  {res.location && <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{res.location}</p>}
                </div>
                <span className="font-bold text-primary-600 dark:text-primary-400">
                  {formatCurrency(res.price)}
                </span>
              </Link>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <ConfirmModal
        isOpen={isDeleteOpen}
        title="Видалити категорію?"
        description={`Ви дійсно бажаєте видалити категорію «${category.name}»?`}
        isLoading={deleteMutation.isPending}
        onConfirm={handleDelete}
        onCancel={() => setIsDeleteOpen(false)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================================
FILE: .\client\src\pages\categories\CategoryFormPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import { useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';
import { ArrowLeft, Save } from 'lucide-react';
import Breadcrumbs from '@/components/common/Breadcrumbs';
import Button from '@/components/common/Button';
import ErrorAlert from '@/components/common/ErrorAlert';
import Input, { Textarea } from '@/components/common/Input';
import Loading from '@/components/common/Loading';
import {
  useCategoryQuery,
  useCreateCategoryMutation,
  useUpdateCategoryMutation,
} from '@/hooks/queries/useCategories';
import { categorySchema, type CategoryFormValues } from '@/schemas/validation';
import { extractApiErrorMessage } from '@/services/api';

export default function CategoryFormPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const isEditMode = Boolean(id);
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const categoryQuery = useCategoryQuery(id);
  const createMutation = useCreateCategoryMutation();
  const updateMutation = useUpdateCategoryMutation(id ?? '');

  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    reset,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<CategoryFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(categorySchema),
    defaultValues: { name: '', description: '' },
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isEditMode && categoryQuery.data) {
      reset({
        name: categoryQuery.data.name,
        description: categoryQuery.data.description ?? '',
      });
    }
  }, [isEditMode, categoryQuery.data, reset]);

  const onSubmit = async (values: CategoryFormValues) => {
    const payload = {
      name: values.name,
      description: values.description || undefined,
    };
    if (isEditMode && id) {
      await updateMutation.mutateAsync(payload);
    } else {
      await createMutation.mutateAsync(payload);
    }
    navigate('/dashboard/categories');
  };

  if (isEditMode && categoryQuery.isLoading) {
    return <Loading text="Завантаження даних категорії..." />;
  }

  const mutationError = createMutation.error || updateMutation.error || categoryQuery.error;
  const isSaving = createMutation.isPending || updateMutation.isPending;

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl">
      <Breadcrumbs
        items={[
          { label: 'Панель керування', to: '/dashboard' },
          { label: 'Категорії', to: '/dashboard/categories' },
          { label: isEditMode ? 'Редагування категорії' : 'Нова категорія' },
        ]}
      />

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
          {isEditMode
            ? `Редагування: ${categoryQuery.data?.name ?? ''}`
            : 'Створення нової категорії'}
        </h1>
        <Link to="/dashboard/categories">
          <Button variant="outline" size="sm">
            <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Назад
          </Button>
        </Link>
      </div>

      {mutationError && (
        <ErrorAlert message={extractApiErrorMessage(mutationError)} className="mb-5" />
      )}

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}
        noValidate
        className="space-y-5 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-xs dark:border-slate-800 dark:bg-slate-900"
      >
        <Input
          label="Назва категорії *"
          placeholder="Конференц-зали"
          error={errors.name?.message}
          {...register('name')}
        />

        <Textarea
          label="Опис категорії"
          rows={4}
          placeholder="Короткий опис призначення цієї категорії..."
          error={errors.description?.message}
          {...register('description')}
        />

        <div className="flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-100 pt-4 dark:border-slate-800">
          <Link to="/dashboard/categories">
            <Button type="button" variant="outline">
              Скасувати
            </Button>
          </Link>
          <Button type="submit" isLoading={isSaving}>
            <Save className="h-4 w-4" />
            {isEditMode ? 'Зберегти зміни' : 'Створити категорію'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: .\client\src\pages\resources\ResourceDetailsPage.tsx
